# Root cleanup — reverted at user request

**Current status: the last root cleanup is undone.** All eight original root items were restored from the verified external ZIP, with no overwrite of existing files. Restored 3,677 files / 310,026,811 bytes; every restored file SHA-256 matches its original recorded digest. The 34 restored website/dist HTML files also match the kept local baseline byte-for-byte. Original directory structure and archived file modification times were restored.

Restored: website/, Changes/, reference/, page-copy/, BUILD-VERIFICATION.md, README-FIRST.md, MANIFEST.sha256 and root package-lock.json. The handoff note, .gitignore, root README and existing next-site source/config/public/baseline files remain unchanged. No React/component conversion, rebuild, checker, deployment or Git command was run during this undo. The external ZIP was retained; previous recycled copies were not emptied or altered.

The restored parent lock may produce the former Next workspace-root warning on a future build. The original-site comparison helpers can again find website/dist. Existing Next build outputs were left as-is.

## Reference workflow and remaining limitations

The original reference remains in the verified external ZIP until production launch. The current browser-checks and computed-style helpers still hardcode ../website/dist and therefore require restoring that archived path (or a separately approved path-configuration change) before old-vs-new suites can run. Generic capture/normalization tools can use caller-supplied restored directories. The local HTML archive alone lacks original assets/fonts for full visual screenshots. No parity, hydration, behavior or computed-style suite was rerun or falsely relabeled as passing after root removal; retained historical numeric logs remain intact.

Existing README/migration summary text describing website/ as an in-repository reference was not silently edited: approval covered backup/root recycling/build/check/report, not documentation/harness rewrites. This report records the new external-archive location and the explicit approved retention-policy exception. Keep the flagged handoff note for unresolved client/SEO decisions. Its proposals were not applied to app copy/routes.

The prior strict background-position representation differences, gallery paint issue and host-only header/308/cleanUrls/cache/MIME/range checks remain outside this cleanup. Forms are still review-only; no deployment or production launch occurred. No sitemap regeneration or package audit fix.

**STOP. Approved root cleanup is complete.**
